# Requirement
```text
Employee Management System
The employee management system manages a named company that owns multiple departments and employs workers, salespeople, and managers.
Every employee stores the department label, name, birth date, and social insurance number.
Workers store weekly working hours and hourly rates.
Off-shift workers additionally store whether they have a weekend permit and whether they have an official holiday permit.
Shift workers additionally store a holiday premium and can only belong to the Delivery department.
Salespeople store a fixed salary, an amount of sales, and a commission percentage.
Managers store a fixed salary, a position title, and a list of direct subordinate employees.
Each department is identified by a department type of PRODUCTION, CONTROL, or DELIVERY, stores its employees, and is controlled by one manager.
The company can calculate the total salary of all employees, the total commission amount of all salespeople, and the total holiday premiums paid to all shift workers. Monetary results are rounded to two decimal places.
A department can calculate the average weekly working hours of its workers and returns 0 when the department has no workers. The average is rounded to two decimal places.
A manager can return the number of direct subordinate employees.
Functional requirement:
1. Calculate the total salary of all employees in the company. Total salary equals the sum of workers' salary, salespeople's salary, and managers' salary. Workers' salary equals weeklyWorkingHour multiplied by hourlyRates, plus holiday premiums for shift workers. Salespeople's salary equals salary plus amountOfSales multiplied by commissionPercentage. Managers' salary equals salary. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
2. Calculate the average weekly working hours for all workers within a given department. The method operates on a Department instance and computes the average of all Worker employees' weeklyWorkingHour in that department's employee list. Return 0 if there are no workers in the department. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
3. Determine the total commission amount for all salespeople in the company. Sum amountOfSales multiplied by commissionPercentage for all salespeople. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
4. Calculate the total holiday premiums paid to all shift workers in the company. Shift workers can only belong to the Delivery department; the setDepartment method of ShiftWorker shall throw an IllegalArgumentException when the department is null or not "Delivery". Retain two decimal places for the return value. Return 0 if there are no shift workers in the company.
5. Get the number of direct subordinate employees for each manager.
```

# Operation Specifications
Company::calculateTotalShiftWorkerHolidayPremiums
Summary: 
Calculates the total holiday premiums paid to all ShiftWorker employees in the company. 
The total is the sum of the holiday premiums of all shift workers. 
Returns 0 if the company has no shift workers. The result is rounded to two decimal places.
Algorithm:
totalPremium = 0.0
FOR each employee IN Company.employees:
  IF employee IS instance of ShiftWorker:
    totalPremium = totalPremium + employee.calculateHolidayPremium()
  END IF
END FOR
RETURN round(totalPremium, 2)
Related Features: 
Company.employees; ShiftWorker.holidayPremium (via calculateHolidayPremium)
Related Classes: Employee, ShiftWorker
Input: None
Output: 
double - the total monetary holiday premium for all shift workers, rounded to two decimal places 
(e.g., 1200.00). Returns 0.0 if the company has no shift workers.
Pre-condition: 
The Company instance exists. The employees list is initialized (non-null).
Post-condition: 
A non-negative double value representing the total holiday premium rounded to two decimal places 
is returned. No state of the company or its employees is modified.

Company::calculateTotalEmployeeSalary
Summary: 
Calculates the total salary of all employees in the company. The total salary 
is the sum of salaries of all Worker employees (OffShiftWorker and ShiftWorker), 
all SalesPeople employees, and all Manager employees. For shift workers, the 
holiday premium is added to the base salary (weeklyWorkingHour * hourlyRates). 
The result is rounded to two decimal places.
Algorithm:
totalSalary = 0.0
FOR each employee IN Company.employees:
  IF employee IS instance of Worker:
    baseSalary = employee.weeklyWorkingHour * employee.hourlyRates
    IF employee IS instance of ShiftWorker:
      baseSalary = baseSalary + employee.calculateHolidayPremium()
    END IF
    totalSalary = totalSalary + baseSalary
  ELSE IF employee IS instance of SalesPeople:
    totalSalary = totalSalary + employee.salary + employee.getTotalCommission()
  ELSE IF employee IS instance of Manager:
    totalSalary = totalSalary + employee.salary
  END IF
END FOR
RETURN round(totalSalary, 2)
Related Features: 
Company.employees; Worker.weeklyWorkingHour; Worker.hourlyRates; 
ShiftWorker.holidayPremium (via calculateHolidayPremium); 
SalesPeople.salary; SalesPeople.amountOfSales; SalesPeople.commissionPercentage; 
Manager.salary
Related Classes: Employee, Worker, ShiftWorker, OffShiftWorker, SalesPeople, Manager
Input: None
Output: 
double - the total monetary salary of all employees in the company, 
rounded to two decimal places (e.g., 12500.75). Returns 0.0 if the company has no employees.
Pre-condition: 
The Company instance exists. The employees list is initialized (non-null). 
All employees referenced have valid salary-related fields.
Post-condition: 
A non-negative double value representing the total salary rounded to two decimal places is returned. 
No state of the company or its employees is modified.

Company::calculateTotalSalesPeopleCommission
Summary: 
Calculates the total commission amount paid to all SalesPeople employees in the company. 
The total commission is the sum of (amountOfSales * commissionPercentage) for each 
SalesPeople. The result is rounded to two decimal places.
Algorithm:
totalCommission = 0.0
FOR each employee IN Company.employees:
  IF employee IS instance of SalesPeople:
    totalCommission = totalCommission + employee.getTotalCommission()
  END IF
END FOR
RETURN round(totalCommission, 2)
Related Features: 
Company.employees; SalesPeople.amountOfSales; SalesPeople.commissionPercentage 
(both accessed through getTotalCommission)
Related Classes: Employee, SalesPeople
Input: None
Output: 
double - the total monetary commission for all salespeople, rounded to two decimal places 
(e.g., 3450.50). Returns 0.0 if the company has no salespeople.
Pre-condition: 
The Company instance exists. The employees list is initialized (non-null).
Post-condition: 
A non-negative double value representing the total commission rounded to two decimal places 
is returned. No state of the company or its employees is modified.

```text
Each department is identified by a department type of PRODUCTION, CONTROL, or DELIVERY, stores its employees, and is controlled by one manager.
The company can calculate the total salary of all employees, the total commission amount of all salespeople, and the total holiday premiums paid to all shift workers. Monetary results are rounded to two decimal places.
A department can calculate the average weekly working hours of its workers and returns 0 when the department has no workers. The average is rounded to two decimal places.
A manager can return the number of direct subordinate employees.
Functional requirement:
1. Calculate the total salary of all employees in the company. Total salary equals the sum of workers' salary, salespeople's salary, and managers' salary. Workers' salary equals weeklyWorkingHour multiplied by hourlyRates, plus holiday premiums for shift workers. Salespeople's salary equals salary plus amountOfSales multiplied by commissionPercentage. Managers' salary equals salary. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
2. Calculate the average weekly working hours for all workers within a given department. The method operates on a Department instance and computes the average of all Worker employees' weeklyWorkingHour in that department's employee list. Return 0 if there are no workers in the department. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
3. Determine the total commission amount for all salespeople in the company. Sum amountOfSales multiplied by commissionPercentage for all salespeople. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
4. Calculate the total holiday premiums paid to all shift workers in the company. Shift workers can only belong to the Delivery department; the setDepartment method of ShiftWorker shall throw an IllegalArgumentException when the department is null or not "Delivery". Retain two decimal places for the return value. Return 0 if there are no shift workers in the company.
5. Get the number of direct subordinate employees for each manager.
```

Manager::getDirectSubordinateEmployeesCount

Pre-condition:

Post-condition: 
A non-negative integer representing the direct subordinate count is returned. 
No state of the manager is modified.

# Operation Specifications
SalesPeople::getTotalCommission
Summary: 
Calculates the total commission earned by this single SalesPeople employee. 
The total commission is calculated as the product of the amount of sales and 
the commission percentage. The result is rounded to two decimal places.
Algorithm:
commission = amountOfSales * commissionPercentage
RETURN round(commission, 2)
Related Features: 
SalesPeople.amountOfSales; SalesPeople.commissionPercentage
Related Classes: None
Input: None
Output: 
double - the monetary commission amount earned by this salesperson, rounded to two decimal 
places (e.g., 1250.50). Returns 0.0 if amountOfSales or commissionPercentage is 0.
Pre-condition: 
The SalesPeople instance exists with valid amountOfSales (>= 0) and commissionPercentage (>= 0) values.
Post-condition: 
A non-negative double value representing the commission rounded to two decimal places is returned. 
No state of the salesperson is modified.

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
Employee Management System
The employee management system manages a named company that owns multiple departments and employs workers, salespeople, and managers.
Every employee stores the department label, name, birth date, and social insurance number.
Workers store weekly working hours and hourly rates.
Off-shift workers additionally store whether they have a weekend permit and whether they have an official holiday permit.
Shift workers additionally store a holiday premium and can only belong to the Delivery department.
Salespeople store a fixed salary, an amount of sales, and a commission percentage.
Managers store a fixed salary, a position title, and a list of direct subordinate employees.
Each department is identified by a department type of PRODUCTION, CONTROL, or DELIVERY, stores its employees, and is controlled by one manager.
The company can calculate the total salary of all employees, the total commission amount of all salespeople, and the total holiday premiums paid to all shift workers. Monetary results are rounded to two decimal places.
A department can calculate the average weekly working hours of its workers and returns 0 when the department has no workers. The average is rounded to two decimal places.
A manager can return the number of direct subordinate employees.
Functional requirement:
1. Calculate the total salary of all employees in the company. Total salary equals the sum of workers' salary, salespeople's salary, and managers' salary. Workers' salary equals weeklyWorkingHour multiplied by hourlyRates, plus holiday premiums for shift workers. Salespeople's salary equals salary plus amountOfSales multiplied by commissionPercentage. Managers' salary equals salary. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
2. Calculate the average weekly working hours for all workers within a given department. The method operates on a Department instance and computes the average of all Worker employees' weeklyWorkingHour in that department's employee list. Return 0 if there are no workers in the department. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
3. Determine the total commission amount for all salespeople in the company. Sum amountOfSales multiplied by commissionPercentage for all salespeople. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
4. Calculate the total holiday premiums paid to all shift workers in the company. Shift workers can only belong to the Delivery department; the setDepartment method of ShiftWorker shall throw an IllegalArgumentException when the department is null or not "Delivery". Retain two decimal places for the return value. Return 0 if there are no shift workers in the company.
5. Get the number of direct subordinate employees for each manager.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title employee Model

class Company {
    name : String
    departments : List<Department>
    employees : List<Employee>
    calculateTotalEmployeeSalary() : double
    calculateTotalSalesPeopleCommission() : double
    calculateTotalShiftWorkerHolidayPremiums() : double
}
class Department {
    type : DepartmentType
    company : Company
    employees : List<Employee>
    manager : Manager
    calculateAverageWorkerWorkingHours() : double
}
abstract class Employee {
    birthDate : EDate
    department : String
    name : String
    socialInsuranceNumber : String
    company : Company
}
class Manager {
    position : String
    salary : double
    subordinates : List<Employee>
    getDirectSubordinateEmployeesCount() : int
}
class OffShiftWorker {
    officialHolidayPermit : boolean
    weekendPermit : boolean
}
class SalesPeople {
    amountOfSales : double
    commissionPercentage : double
    salary : double
    getTotalCommission() : double
}
class ShiftWorker {
    holidayPremium : double
    calculateHolidayPremium() : double
    setdepartment(department : String) : void
}
abstract class Worker {
    hourlyRates : double
    weeklyWorkingHour : int
}
enum DepartmentType {
    PRODUCTION
    CONTROL
    DELIVERY
}

Company *-- "*" Department : departments  'opposite: company
Company *-- "*" Employee : employees  'opposite: company
Department --> Company : company  'opposite: departments
Department --> "*" Employee : employees
Department --> Manager : manager
Employee --> Company : company  'opposite: employees
Manager --|> Employee
Manager --> "*" Employee : subordinates
OffShiftWorker --|> Worker
SalesPeople --|> Employee
ShiftWorker --|> Worker
Worker --|> Employee
@enduml
```

The result is rounded to two decimal places.
Algorithm:
  totalSalary = 0
  for each employee in company.employees:
    if employee is Worker:
      workerSalary = employee.weeklyWorkingHour * employee.hourlyRates
      if employee is ShiftWorker:
        workerSalary = workerSalary + employee.holidayPremium
      totalSalary = totalSalary + workerSalary
    else if employee is SalesPeople:
      salespersonSalary = employee.salary + (employee.amountOfSales * employee.commissionPercentage)
      totalSalary = totalSalary + salespersonSalary
    else if employee is Manager:
      totalSalary = totalSalary + employee.salary
  return round(totalSalary, 2)

# Requirement
```text
Employee Management System
The employee management system manages a named company that owns multiple departments and employs workers, salespeople, and managers.
Every employee stores the department label, name, birth date, and social insurance number.
Workers store weekly working hours and hourly rates.
Off-shift workers additionally store whether they have a weekend permit and whether they have an official holiday permit.
Shift workers additionally store a holiday premium and can only belong to the Delivery department.
Salespeople store a fixed salary, an amount of sales, and a commission percentage.
Managers store a fixed salary, a position title, and a list of direct subordinate employees.
Each department is identified by a department type of PRODUCTION, CONTROL, or DELIVERY, stores its employees, and is controlled by one manager.
The company can calculate the total salary of all employees, the total commission amount of all salespeople, and the total holiday premiums paid to all shift workers. Monetary results are rounded to two decimal places.
A department can calculate the average weekly working hours of its workers and returns 0 when the department has no workers. The average is rounded to two decimal places.
A manager can return the number of direct subordinate employees.
Functional requirement:
1. Calculate the total salary of all employees in the company. Total salary equals the sum of workers' salary, salespeople's salary, and managers' salary. Workers' salary equals weeklyWorkingHour multiplied by hourlyRates, plus holiday premiums for shift workers. Salespeople's salary equals salary plus amountOfSales multiplied by commissionPercentage. Managers' salary equals salary. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
2. Calculate the average weekly working hours for all workers within a given department. The method operates on a Department instance and computes the average of all Worker employees' weeklyWorkingHour in that department's employee list. Return 0 if there are no workers in the department. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
3. Determine the total commission amount for all salespeople in the company. Sum amountOfSales multiplied by commissionPercentage for all salespeople. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
4. Calculate the total holiday premiums paid to all shift workers in the company. Shift workers can only belong to the Delivery department; the setDepartment method of ShiftWorker shall throw an IllegalArgumentException when the department is null or not "Delivery". Retain two decimal places for the return value. Return 0 if there are no shift workers in the company.
5. Get the number of direct subordinate employees for each manager.
```

# Operation Specifications
Department::calculateAverageWorkerWorkingHours
Summary: Calculates the average weekly working hours of all workers (both off-shift and shift workers) within the department. Returns 0 if the department has no workers. The result is rounded to two decimal places.
Algorithm:
  totalHours = 0
  workerCount = 0
  for each employee in department.employees:
    if employee is Worker:
      totalHours = totalHours + employee.weeklyWorkingHour
      workerCount = workerCount + 1
  if workerCount == 0:
    return 0.0
  average = totalHours / workerCount
  return round(average, 2)
Related Features: employees
Related Classes: Employee, Worker
Input: None
Output: double - the average weekly working hours of workers in the department, rounded to two decimal places (value range: >= 0.0; returns 0.0 when no workers exist)
Pre-condition: The Department instance must be properly initialized with a non-null employees list containing valid Worker objects with weeklyWorkingHour attribute set.
Post-condition: Returns the average weekly working hours as a double value rounded to two decimal places (or 0.0 if no workers exist); the department's state remains unchanged.

# Requirement
```text
Employee Management System
The employee management system manages a named company that owns multiple departments and employs workers, salespeople, and managers.
Every employee stores the department label, name, birth date, and social insurance number.
Workers store weekly working hours and hourly rates.
Off-shift workers additionally store whether they have a weekend permit and whether they have an official holiday permit.
Shift workers additionally store a holiday premium and can only belong to the Delivery department.
Salespeople store a fixed salary, an amount of sales, and a commission percentage.
Managers store a fixed salary, a position title, and a list of direct subordinate employees.
Each department is identified by a department type of PRODUCTION, CONTROL, or DELIVERY, stores its employees, and is controlled by one manager.
The company can calculate the total salary of all employees, the total commission amount of all salespeople, and the total holiday premiums paid to all shift workers. Monetary results are rounded to two decimal places.
A department can calculate the average weekly working hours of its workers and returns 0 when the department has no workers. The average is rounded to two decimal places.
A manager can return the number of direct subordinate employees.
Functional requirement:
1. Calculate the total salary of all employees in the company. Total salary equals the sum of workers' salary, salespeople's salary, and managers' salary. Workers' salary equals weeklyWorkingHour multiplied by hourlyRates, plus holiday premiums for shift workers. Salespeople's salary equals salary plus amountOfSales multiplied by commissionPercentage. Managers' salary equals salary. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
2. Calculate the average weekly working hours for all workers within a given department. The method operates on a Department instance and computes the average of all Worker employees' weeklyWorkingHour in that department's employee list. Return 0 if there are no workers in the department. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
3. Determine the total commission amount for all salespeople in the company. Sum amountOfSales multiplied by commissionPercentage for all salespeople. Retain two decimal places for the return value.
4. Calculate the total holiday premiums paid to all shift workers in the company. Shift workers can only belong to the Delivery department; the setDepartment method of ShiftWorker shall throw an IllegalArgumentException when the department is null or not "Delivery". Retain two decimal places for the return value. Return 0 if there are no shift workers in the company.
5. Get the number of direct subordinate employees for each manager.
```

# Operation Specifications
SalesPeople::getTotalCommission
Summary: Calculates and returns the total commission earned by this salesperson, which is the product of their amount of sales and commission percentage. The result is rounded to two decimal places.
Algorithm:
  commission = amountOfSales * commissionPercentage
  return round(commission, 2)
Related Features: amountOfSales, commissionPercentage
Related Classes: None
Input: None
Output: double - the commission amount for this salesperson, rounded to two decimal places (value range: >= 0.0)
Pre-condition: The SalesPeople instance must be properly initialized with valid amountOfSales (>= 0.0) and commissionPercentage (>= 0.0) attributes.
Post-condition: Returns the commission amount as a double value rounded to two decimal places; the salesperson's state remains unchanged.
